## 創建 LRS 交叉截面( 利納參考工具) · 使用率

**id**　aa2c63875162ad6d12-2

**english**　Create LRS Intersection

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 引用工具箱
- 配置工具et
- LRS 跨部門工具

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/create-lrs-intersection.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/create-lrs-intersection.html

**text**　相交叉特征類的預測協調系統必須與父 LRS 網絡參數值匹配。父LRS 網絡參數值可以用作一個交叉層,因此產生的交叉點將在網絡路線之間產生。父 LRS 網絡參數值不能是一個特性服務。交叉層必須和父 LRS 網絡參數值位于同一地理資料庫中。交叉點的名稱是通過將網絡描述欄位參數值與交叉層參數中的描述欄位值混為一體而創建的,該數值由交叉層參數中的名稱分隔符值分開。交叉層必須是 Z 啟用的。只有逗號()才允許作為共號。路由路由交叉路口的命名分隔符。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "The projected coordinate system of the intersecting feature classes must match that of the Parent LRS Network parameter value.\nThe Parent LRS Network parameter value can be used as an intersecting layer, so the intersections produced will be between the routes in a network.\nThe Parent LRS Network parameter value cannot be a feature service.\nThe intersecting layers must be in the same geodatabase as the Parent LRS Network parameter value.\nThe name of the intersection is created by concatenating the Network Description Field parameter value with the Description Field value in the Intersecting Layers parameter, separated by the Name Separator value in the Intersecting Layers parameter.\nThe intersecting layers must be z-enabled.\nOnly a comma (,) is allowed as a name separator for route-on-route intersections.", "section": "Usage"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/linear-referencing/create-lrs-intersection.html#usage

## 創建 LRS 交叉截面( 利納參考工具) · 代碼樣本

**id**　aa2c63875162ad6d12-20

**english**　Create LRS Intersection

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 引用工具箱
- 配置工具et
- LRS 跨部門工具

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/create-lrs-intersection.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/create-lrs-intersection.html

**text**　# Name: Create_LRS_Intersection_ex1.py
# Description: Create a new LRS Intersection registered to an LRS Network in the Python window.
# Requires: ArcGIS Linear Referencing

# Local variables
parent_network = r"C:\Demo.gdb\LRS\Network1"
network_description_field = "RouteName"
intersection_feature_class_name = "Intersection"
intersecting_layers = "Boundary CountyName CountyID '& County:'"
consider_z = "DO_NOT_CONSIDER_Z"
z_tolerance = ""

# Set current workspace
arcpy.env.workspace = r"C:\Demo.gdb"

# Run the tool
arcpy.lr.CreateLRSIntersection(parent_network, network_description_field, intersection_feature_class_name, intersecting_layers, consider_z, z_tolerance)

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "# Name: Create_LRS_Intersection_ex1.py\n# Description: Create a new LRS Intersection registered to an LRS Network in the Python window.\n# Requires: ArcGIS Linear Referencing\n\n# Local variables\nparent_network = r\"C:\\Demo.gdb\\LRS\\Network1\"\nnetwork_description_field = \"RouteName\"\nintersection_feature_class_name = \"Intersection\"\nintersecting_layers = \"Boundary CountyName CountyID '& County:'\"\nconsider_z = \"DO_NOT_CONSIDER_Z\"\nz_tolerance = \"\"\n\n# Set current workspace\narcpy.env.workspace = r\"C:\\Demo.gdb\"\n\n# Run the tool\narcpy.lr.CreateLRSIntersection(parent_network, network_description_field, intersection_feature_class_name, intersecting_layers, consider_z, z_tolerance)", "section": "Code sample"}

## 創建 LRS 交叉截面( 利納參考工具) · 代碼樣本

**id**　aa2c63875162ad6d12-22

**english**　Create LRS Intersection

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 引用工具箱
- 配置工具et
- LRS 跨部門工具

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/create-lrs-intersection.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/create-lrs-intersection.html

**text**　# Name: Create_LRS_Intersection_ex2.py
# Description: Create a new LRS Intersection registered to an LRS Network.
# Requires: ArcGIS Linear Referencing

# Import arcpy module
import arcpy

# Local variables
parent_network = r"C:\Demo.gdb\LRS\NonLineNetwork"
network_description_field = "RouteName"
intersection_feature_class_name = "Intersection"
intersecting_layers = "NonLineNetwork RouteName RouteName '& RouteName:'"
consider_z = "DO_NOT_CONSIDER_Z"
z_tolerance = ""

# Set current workspace
arcpy.env.workspace = r"C:\Demo.gdb"

# Run the tool
arcpy.lr.CreateLRSIntersection(parent_network, network_description_field, intersection_feature_class_name, intersecting_layers, consider_z, z_tolerance)

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "parent_network", "label_zh": "LRS 家長網絡", "label_en": "Parent LRS Network", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The network to which the intersection will be registered."}, {"parameter": "network_description_field", "label_zh": "網絡描述欄位", "label_en": "Network Description Field", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "The field in the network layer that will be used to name the intersections with other intersecting layers."}, {"parameter": "intersection_feature_class_name", "label_zh": "交交交間特征類", "label_en": "Intersection Feature Class", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The name of the new intersection point feature class."}, {"parameter": "intersecting_layers", "label_zh": "相交層", "label_en": "Intersecting Layers", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "The feature class that intersects the LRS Network and contains the following information: Value table columns: Intersection Layer—The feature class that intersects the LRS Network. ID Field—The field in the intersecting layer used to uniquely identify the feature that intersects the network. Description Field—The field that provides the description, such as town or county name, of the intersecting feature. Name Separator—The name separator for the intersection, such as AND, INTERSECT, +, or |."}, {"parameter": "consider_z", "label_zh": "生成交叉點時考慮z值( 可選)", "label_en": "Consider z-values when generating intersections (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether z-values will be used when generating intersections. Checked—Z-values will be used when generating intersections. Unchecked—Z-values will not be used when generating intersections. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "z_tolerance", "label_zh": "容忍(可選擇)", "label_en": "Z Tolerance (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The z-tolerance that will be used to generate intersections."}]

**signatures**

## 創建 LRS 交叉截面( 利納參考工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 引用工具箱
- 配置工具et
- LRS 跨部門工具

**english**　Create LRS Intersection

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/create-lrs-intersection.html

**id**　aa2c63875162ad6d12-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/create-lrs-intersection.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Create LRS Intersection，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。LRS 家長網絡 [parent_network]、網絡描述欄位 [network_description_field]、交交交間特征類 [intersection_feature_class_name]、相交層 [intersecting_layers]、生成交叉點時考慮z值( 可選) [consider_z]、容忍(可選擇) [z_tolerance]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/linear-referencing/create-lrs-intersection.html#parameters

Error posting version GDB.editorversion. Operation can only be executed by the owner since the version is protected.
默認版本受保護且當前用戶沒有足夠權限提交版本時會收到此錯誤。
有關詳細信息，請參閱以下主題：
管理分支版本 > 保護默認版本
管理傳統版本 > 保護默認版本

## 網格轉多點 (3D Analyst 工具) · 用法

**id**　aa2e0eeaf05dff2790-2

**english**　Raster To Multipoint

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 3D Analyst 工具箱
- 網格工具集
- 轉換工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/raster-to-multipoint.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/raster-to-multipoint.html

**text**　如果擁有網格高程資料并需要訪問表面三角化提供的實用功能，請考慮使用此工具，因為多點圖徵可加載到 TIN 或 Terrain 資料集中。
當輸入網格的參數值過大時，請考慮應用細化方法參數減少導出至多點圖徵類的像元數。 選項如下： z 容差 -細化像元的同時保持垂直精度。 核 -細化像元的同時控制水平采樣距離。 VIP -當生成的多點主要應用于可視化應用程序時，請使用此方法。 此方法相對較快，能夠輸出可預測點數，并識別局部山峰和凹地。 然而，該方法對噪點敏感，可能會忽略跨越范圍大于 3 像元 × 3 像元的地形圖徵。 VIP 直方圖 -使用此選項作為應用 VIP 選項的初始步驟，它會生成顯著性得分的直方圖，反映隨每個遞增的百分比值而將要選擇的點數。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/3d-analyst/raster-to-multipoint.html#usage

## 網格轉多點 (3D Analyst 工具) · 參數

**id**　aa2e0eeaf05dff2790-7

**english**　Raster To Multipoint

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 3D Analyst 工具箱
- 網格工具集
- 轉換工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/raster-to-multipoint.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/raster-to-multipoint.html

**parameter**　method

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 細化方法 （可選）
- 指定要應用于輸入網格的細化方法，以選擇要導出至多點圖徵類的像元的子集。 No Thinning—No thinning will be applied. This is the default. Z Tolerance—Only the cells that are required for maintaining a surface within a specified z-range of the input raster will be exported. Kernel—The raster will be divided into equal-sized tiles based on the Thinning Value parameter value, and one or two cells that meet the Kernel Method parameter value will be exported. VIP—A roving 3-cell-by-3-cell window will be used to create a three-dimensional best fit plane. Each cell is given a significance score based on its absolute deviation from this plane. A histogram of these scores is then used to determine the cells that will be exported based on the Thinning Value parameter value. VIP Histogram—A table will be created containing the significance values and the corresponding number of points associated with those values.
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

- 指定在輸入網格應用核細化時在每個核鄰域中使用的選擇方法。 Minimum—A point will be created at the cell with the smallest elevation value found in the kernel neighborhood. This is the default. Maximum—A point will be created at the cell with the largest elevation value found in the kernel neighborhood. Minimum and Maximum—Two points will be created at the cells with the smallest and largest z-values found in the kernel neighborhood. ## 網格轉多點 (3D Analyst 工具) · 參數

**id**　aa2e0eeaf05dff2790-15

**english**　Raster To Multipoint

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 3D Analyst 工具箱
- 網格工具集
- 轉換工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/raster-to-multipoint.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/raster-to-multipoint.html

**parameter**　method

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- method （可選）
- 指定要應用于輸入網格的細化方法，以選擇要導出至多點圖徵類的像元的子集。 NO_THIN—No thinning will be applied. This is the default. ZTOLERANCE—Only the cells that are required for maintaining a surface within a specified z-range of the input raster will be exported. KERNEL—The raster will be divided into equal-sized tiles based on the Thinning Value parameter value, and one or two cells that meet the Kernel Method parameter value will be exported. VIP—A roving 3-cell-by-3-cell window will be used to create a three-dimensional best fit plane. Each cell is given a significance score based on its absolute deviation from this plane. A histogram of these scores is then used to determine the cells that will be exported based on the Thinning Value parameter value. VIP_HISTOGRAM—A table will be created containing the significance values and the corresponding number of points associated with those values.
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/3d-analyst/raster-to-multipoint.html#parameters

## 網格轉多點 (3D Analyst 工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　aa2e0eeaf05dff2790-22

**english**　Raster To Multipoint

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 3D Analyst 工具箱
- 網格工具集
- 轉換工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/raster-to-multipoint.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/raster-to-multipoint.html

**text**　'''*********************************************************************
Name: RasterToMultipoint Example
Description: This script demonstrates how to use
             the RasterToMultipoint tool to create multipoint datasets
             fot all IMG rasters in a target workspace.
**********************************************************************'''
# Import system modules
import arcpy

# Set default workspace
arcpy.env.workspace = "C:/data"
# Create the list of IMG rasters
rasterList = arcpy.ListRasters("*", "IMG")
# Loop the process for each raster
if rasterList:
    for raster in rasterList:
        # Set Local Variables
        # [:-4] strips the last 4 characters (.img) from the raster name
        outTbl = "VIP_" + raster[:-4] + ".dbf"
        method = "VIP_HISTOGRAM"
        zfactor = 1
        # Run RasterToMultipoint
        arcpy.ddd.RasterToMultipoint(raster, "", outTbl, method, "", zfactor)
else:
    print("There are no IMG rasters in the " + arcpy.env.workspace + " directory.")

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/3d-analyst/raster-to-multipoint.html#parameters

## 網格轉多點 (3D Analyst 工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 3D Analyst 工具箱
- 網格工具集
- 轉換工具集

**english**　Raster To Multipoint

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/raster-to-multipoint.html

**id**　aa2e0eeaf05dff2790-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/raster-to-multipoint.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Raster To Multipoint，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入網格 [in_raster]、輸出圖徵類 [out_feature_class]、輸出 VIP 表 （可選） [out_vip_table]、細化方法 （可選） [method]、核方法 （可選） [kernel_method]、Z 因子 （可選） [z_factor]、細化值 （可選） [thinning_value]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/3d-analyst/raster-to-multipoint.html#parameters

**english**　Raster To Multipoint

**id**　aa2e0eeaf05dff2790-schema

**kind**　版本參數表

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/raster-to-multipoint.html#parameters

**source_language**　en

**content_version**　3.8

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/3d-analyst/raster-to-multipoint.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_raster", "label_zh": "輸入網格", "label_en": "Input Raster", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Raster Layer; Mosaic Layer", "description_en": "The raster that will be processed."}, {"parameter": "out_feature_class", "label_zh": "輸出圖徵類", "label_en": "Output Feature Class", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Class", "description_en": "The feature class that will be produced."}, {"parameter": "out_vip_table", "label_zh": "輸出 VIP 表 （可選）", "label_en": "Output VIP table (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Table", "description_en": "The histogram table that will be created when VIP Histogram is specified for the Thinning Method parameter. The histogram table that will be created when VIP_HISTOGRAM is specified for the method parameter."}, {"parameter": "method", "label_zh": "細化方法 （可選）", "label_en": "Thinning Method (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the thinning method that will be applied to the input raster to select a subset of cells that will be exported to the multipoint feature class. No Thinning—No thinning will be applied. This is the default. Z Tolerance—Only the cells that are required for maintaining a surface within a specified z-range of the input raster will be exported. Kernel—The raster will be divided into equal-sized tiles based on the Thinning Value parameter value, and one or two cells that meet the Kernel Method parameter value will be exported. VIP—A roving 3-cell-by-3-cell window will be used to create a three-dimensional best fit plane. Each cell is given a significance score based on its absolute deviation from this plane. A histogram of these scores is then used to determine the cells that will be exported based on the Thinning Value parameter value. VIP Histogram—A table will be created containing the significance values and the corresponding number of points associated with those values."}, {"parameter": "kernel_method", "label_zh": "核方法 （可選）", "label_en": "Kernel Method (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the selection method that will be used in each kernel neighborhood when kernel thinning is applied on the input raster. Minimum—A point will be created at the cell with the smallest elevation value found in the kernel neighborhood. This is the default. Maximum—A point will be created at the cell with the largest elevation value found in the kernel neighborhood. Minimum and Maximum—Two points will be created at the cells with the smallest and largest z-values found in the kernel neighborhood. Closest to Mean—A point will be created at the cell whose elevation value is closest to the average of the cells in the kernel neighborhood."}, {"parameter": "z_factor", "label_zh": "Z 因子 （可選）", "label_en": "Z Factor (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The factor by which z-values will be multiplied. This is typically used to convert z linear units to match x,y linear units. The default is 1, which leaves elevation values unchanged. This parameter is not available if the spatial reference of the input surface has a z-datum with a specified linear unit."}, {"parameter": "thinning_value", "label_zh": "細化值 （可選）", "label_en": "Thinning Value (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The thinning value associated with the Thinning Method parameter value. Z Tolerance—The maximum allowable difference in z-units between the input raster and the surface created from the output multipoint feature class. The default value is one-tenth of the z-range of the input raster. Kernel—The number of raster cells along the edge of each tile. The default value is 3, which means that the raster will be divided into 3-cell-by-3-cell windows. VIP—The percentile rank of the histogram of significance scores. The default value is 5.0, which means that the cells with scores within the top 5 percent of the histogram will be exported."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.3d.RasterToMultipoint(in_raster, out_feature_class, {out_vip_table}, {method}, {kernel_method}, {z_factor}, {thinning_value})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_raster、out_feature_class、out_vip_table、method、kernel_method、z_factor、thinning_value。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。
